# Aureum White-Hat Output — Findings Ledger

The single, append-only record of every white-hat finding across all passes.
One row per finding. Driven by `AUREUM_WHITEHAT_PROCESS.md`; every step's
attestation either adds rows here or records "no finding" in the coverage table
below.

Append-only: never delete a row. A finding that turns out to be a false
positive is marked `False-positive` in **Status**, with the reason in
**Explanation** — the row stays as a record that the surface was checked.

---

## Column legend

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **ID** | `F-NN`, monotonic across the whole project (never reused). |
| **Date** | ISO date the finding was raised (`YYYY-MM-DD`). |
| **Stage/Step** | The white-hat step that surfaced it, e.g. `WI.3`. "Stage that figured it out." |
| **File** | Contract path, e.g. `src/token/AuMM.sol`. |
| **Routine** | Function / modifier / library routine where the issue lives. |
| **Surface** | Catalog ID `S0`–`S12` (see process doc §7). |
| **Severity** | `Critical` / `High` / `Medium` / `Low` / `Info`. |
| **Confidence** | `Confirmed` (full PoC) / `Suspected` (needs PoC) / `Theoretical`. |
| **Issue** | One-line statement of the problem. |
| **Explanation** | The attack path / mechanism, and — once resolved — the resolution reasoning. |
| **Status** | `Open` / `Confirmed` / `Fixed` / `Accepted-risk` / `False-positive`. |
| **Fix** | Commit hash of the fix (or `—`), plus byte-identity note if the fix touches the substrate seam. |
| **PoC** | Path to the Foundry test that proves/kills it, e.g. `test/whitehat/F07_emaDonation.t.sol`. |

### Status lifecycle
```
Open ─▶ Confirmed ─▶ Fixed
  │          │
  └─▶ False-positive   └─▶ Accepted-risk (documented, no code change)
```
A `Suspected` finding stays **Open** until a PoC moves it to **Confirmed** or
**False-positive**. Nothing reaches **Fixed** without both a PoC and a fix
commit.

| F-01 | 2026-06-11 | WK.10 | `src/governance/AureumGovernance.sol` | `_voteSucceeded` / `_createProposal` / `castVote` | S9 | Medium | Confirmed | Quorum denominator is the propose-time `VOTING_WEIGHT.totalSupply()` snapshot (L201/L204→L289) while `forVotes`/`againstVotes` accrue at vote-time (`poke`+`governanceWeight`, L257-258); turnout can exceed 100% of snapshot, so the 20% quorum (`QUORUM_BPS`) gates against a stale, attacker-shrinkable base, not true eligible weight. | Root cause: `VotingWeight._totalQualifiedWeight` is a lazy poke-accumulator (VotingWeight L95-96/L115-120) excluding never-poked holders; the proposer is not poked in `_createProposal` (L196-206) but self-pokes in `castVote` (L257), so their weight is absent from the denominator yet present in the numerator. Effective quorum = 20% of poked weight; at `snapshot==0` it is vacuous. A single qualified holder unilaterally passes gauge revoke / fee change (simple majority) and composition swap (2/3). Bounded by the 14-day qualification cliff (VotingWeight L137, no flash weight), the non-refundable 1,000 svZCHF deposit, the ~16-day vote+timelock contest window (one larger honest against-vote defeats it), permissionless defensive poking, and the WK.8 bounded blast radius (no theft, reversible). Sharpest in early/thin-participation life. Fix: read the quorum denominator at tally-time `endBlock` (all self-poked voters then included → turnout ≤ 100%), or Governor-style historical weight checkpoints. WK.13 intent: a DEVIATION from the documented `vetoSupport ≤ totalSupply` invariant (VotingWeight L13/L21), not an I-D17-intended reuse — I-D17 deferred the semantics to Stage K (STAGE_I_NOTES L179) and the original consumer `vetoProposal` reads `totalSupply()` live (VaultClassRegistry L279); AureumGovernance's propose-time snapshot is the divergence → CODE FIX (not Accepted-risk). RESOLVED (e6d9b3d) — `_voteSucceeded` reads the quorum denominator LIVE at tally via `VOTING_WEIGHT.totalSupply()` (L294), the WK.13 `vetoProposal`-mirroring alignment: by `endBlock` every voter has self-poked so the accumulator subsumes the numerator → turnout ≤ 100%; `snapshotTotalSupply` retained for the `ProposalCreated` event only. Regression `test_F01_liveTallySupplyDefeatsStaleQuorumGaming` models the accumulator growing 20× post-propose (snapshot 10_000e18 → live 200_000e18) — a 10_000e18 minority vote the stale snapshot passed is now Defeated; F01 suite 5/5. No substrate seam. | Fixed | `e6d9b3d` (no substrate seam) | `test/whitehat/F01_quorumSnapshotTiming.t.sol` (5/5) |
| F-02 | 2026-06-11 | WK.12 | `src/governance/VotingWeight.sol` | `_positionPower` / `poke` | S9 | Medium | Confirmed | F-9 per-position power curve `base.powDown(e)` (e=0.25 Era0 / ⅓ Era1+, L149-152) is subadditive, so splitting one LP position across N addresses multiplies summed `governanceWeight` by ≈N^(1-e) (N^¾ Era0 / N^⅔ Era1+) at only gas+time cost — defeating the F-9 anti-plutocracy dampening and amplifying governance capture (compounds F-01). | Concavity x^e (e<1, f(0)=0) is subadditive: Σf(pieces) ≥ f(Σ); value conserved across split (`tvl·Σlp_i/totalLP = tvl·lp/totalLP`, L142) but summed power amplified; cliff (L137) / on-ramp (L144-147) / gauge-gate (L133) identical across pieces so they cancel; concavity applied per-(pool,holder) summed per holder (poke L110-112), no cross-address aggregation (impossible on-chain w/o identity). PoC: 16-split → ≈8×, 81-split → ≈27× the weight of one consolidated position of identical total LP. Bounded by per-address gas, 14-day cliff (real non-flash capital), value/base dust floor (L143/L148); no on-chain Sybil resistance; bounded blast radius (no theft, reversible). Fix: apply the root to pool aggregate value distributed pro-rata by LP share (split-invariant), or accept the known concave-voting Sybil tradeoff. Intent (F-9 spec acceptance, `11_formulas.md` F-9 / I-D7) → WH-F + spec check. RESOLVED (a5a3a1b + aumm-site 50e21a5) — spec-first per the §12 ambiguity-gate: canonical F-9 rewritten to the pool-aggregate root (aumm-site `11_formulas.md` section swap + 04_tokenomics/10_constitution/12_glossary/17_faq consistency sweep, 50e21a5), then `VotingWeight._positionPower` rewritten to `tvlEMA(pool)^era-root × (userLP/poolTotalLP) × min(time,ON_RAMP)/ON_RAMP` — the holder leg is linear, so wallet-splitting is weight-invariant by construction (rounding-down sums split pieces to consolidated-minus-dust: 16-split −12 wei, 81-split −88 wei observed). Subadditivity migrates to the gauge-gated pool axis (≤ roster size, requires real diversified LP — incentive-aligned, spec-accepted residual). Spec also locks: time factor linear outside the root; share denominator = total LP (forfeited weight vanishes). PoC flipped from proving the ≈8×/≈27× gain to proving invariance (`test_F02_Split16/81_WeightInvariant_NeverGains` + baseline, 3/3 green); unit recalibration 2 literals (32/32); F04 ratio suite stable (4/4); regression 778/778 unit + 94/94 fork. | Fixed | `a5a3a1b` + aumm-site spec `50e21a5` (no substrate seam) | `test/whitehat/F02_concaveSplitSybil.t.sol` (3/3) |
| F-03 | 2026-06-12 | WK.17 | `script/DeployStageK.s.sol` | `_wire` | S10 | Low | Confirmed | The K7 deploy `_wire` (8 wiring calls, L147-167) never invokes `tvlOracle.setMiliariumRegistry(miliariumRegistry)`, so the K6 Miliarium enumeration leg stays permanently dormant in production and the deployer-pinned one-shot `registrySetter` is never sealed — a deviation from K-D8 "registry bound post-deploy at K7". | K6 (WK.15) added `setMiliariumRegistry` (one-shot self-seal, `registrySetter`-gated, `TVLOracle.sol` L193) + the Leg-2 dense enumeration in `_constellationRatio` (L226-234), guarded by `registryBound = address(miliariumRegistry) != address(0)` (L211). K-D8 specifies binding "post-deploy at K7" and `TVLOracle.sol` L50 NatSpec says bound "at the Stage K (K7) handoff", but the materialized K14 wiring inventory enumerates 8 calls (setVotingWeight / addAuthorizedDonator / 2× setMintRouter / setMinter / 2× setGovernanceContract / setAuthorizer) and `_wire` implements exactly those 8 — `setMiliariumRegistry` is in neither; grep confirms ZERO `tvlOracle.setMiliariumRegistry` calls across `script/` and the K fork tests. Run as-is, `tvlOracle.miliariumRegistry()` stays `address(0)`, Leg 2 is skipped (L226 "dormant until the registry is bound"), and `_constellationRatio` runs the pre-K6 reverse-map leg only → K6 valuation densification is inert and gauged Miliarium pools under-weight their LPs in `VotingWeight.tvl(pool)`. Security corollary: `registrySetter` (L54, deployer-pinned, self-zeros only on first bind) stays unsealed — a lingering one-shot privileged setter; deployer-key compromise before any bind lets an attacker bind a malicious `IMiliariumRegistry` skewing `_constellationRatio` → `tvl()` → governance weight + CCB emission (compounds the WK.15 SUSPECTED spot-tvl item; the K6 seal was the intended closure). Bounded — no value-theft path, valuation-accuracy/liveness only, recoverable while the deployer holds the unsealed setter, and `addConstellationPool` roster population is itself deferred so launch `tvl()` is approximate regardless. Disposition routed (build-line decision, F-01/WK.13 pattern): CODE FIX = add a 9th `_wire` call `tvlOracle.setMiliariumRegistry(IMiliariumRegistry(env MILIARIUM_REGISTRY))` (registrySetter-gated not governance-gated, order-independent of the handoff; needs K governor == the Stage-H TVLOracle deployer, which holds in the single-GOVERNANCE_MULTISIG chain) + a deploy-replay regression asserting `tvlOracle.miliariumRegistry() == MILIARIUM_REGISTRY` post-deploy; ALTERNATIVELY a documented conscious deferral pairing the bind with the deferred `addConstellationPool` roster-population stage. RESOLVED (1e84bfb) — `_wire` gains a 9th call `tvlOracle.setMiliariumRegistry(IMiliariumRegistry(env MILIARIUM_REGISTRY))` after the gauge+Miliarium handoff, before `setAuthorizer` (L166-167): the K6 Leg-2 enumeration binds (`registryBound==true`) and the one-shot `registrySetter` self-seals on deploy. Regression `test_F03_tvlOracleMiliariumRegistryBoundAndSealed` asserts `miliariumRegistry()==MILIARIUM_REGISTRY` + `registrySetter()==address(0)` post-deploy; `DeployStageK` fork suite 6/6. Script + fork test only, no src contract change. | Fixed | `1e84bfb` (no substrate seam) | `test/fork/DeployStageK.t.sol` (6/6) |
| F-04 | 2026-06-12 | WK.18 | `src/governance/VotingWeight.sol` | `_positionPower` / `poke` (consuming `TVLOracle.tvl`) | S7 | Low | Confirmed | `_positionPower` consumes `ORACLE.tvl(pool)` as an atomic SPOT read (L142; `TVLOracle.tvl` L272-283 = live `balancesLiveScaled18 × _constellationRatio`, no oracle-side EMA/TWAP), so a same-tx spot-tvl pump inflates `governanceWeight` with zero change to the hook-gated recorder position — structurally manipulable, though the F-9 power root caps the gain at the 4th/3rd root of the pump. | `value = ORACLE.tvl(pool) × userLP/poolTotalLP` (L142); the recorder shares (L138/L140) are hook-gated (`recordDeposit`/`recordWithdrawal`, `onlyAuMTContract`) and non-flash-movable (OQ-25 cancels the LP-share leg — a normal add-liquidity raises tvl AND poolTotalLP), but `tvl(pool)` is a SPOT read with the EMA living consumer-side (EMASampler, Stage F) NOT in `tvl()`. So spot-tvl is the lone atomically-manipulable input: an attacker holding a qualified position pumps `tvl(pool)` in the same tx as `poke`/`castVote` (flash-swap skewing a thin constellation venue's `balZ/balU` per `_venueRatio` L247-264, or a one-sided DONATION raising the target's balances), captures inflated `governanceWeight`, unwinds. PoC `test/whitehat/F04_spotTvlManipulation.t.sol` 3/3 green proves BOTH halves on the K3 mock harness with a share-1 qualified position (base == tvl): (1) a pure spot-tvl change with ZERO recorder change moves weight (`assertGt(w16, w1)` — the structural exposure), and (2) the F-9 root self-limits it — 16× spot-tvl → ~2× weight Era0 (`powDown(0.25e18)`, 16^¼=2), 8× → ~2× Era1+ (`ONE/3`, 8^⅓=2), 10000× → ~10× (the quartic ceiling at extreme pump). Economically self-limiting: weight-gain = (tvl-pump)^¼ and every pump costs ≥ the pumped value (DONATION 1:1 permanent; flash-SWAP ≥ round-trip slippage+fees) → quartically sublinear ROI. Residual marginal threat = a THIN-venue oracle pump in a SPARSE constellation roster (few `_constellationRatio` averaging venues + deep target) where the slippage-to-gain ratio improves — bounded but not killed, and COMPOUNDS F-01 (quorum-gaming) + F-02 (Sybil-split) since the prize is governance capture not token profit. Bounded: no theft, no flash weight (14-day cliff), recorder-share honesty, ~16-day vote+timelock public contest. Supersedes the WK.15 "if confirmed → F-03" forward-ref (F-03 taken by the WK.17 deploy-omission). Disposition routed (build-line/WH-H decision, F-01/WK.13 pattern): CODE FIX = oracle-side EMA/TWAP on `TVLOracle.tvl` (the H-D9 spot-vs-EMA design — Stage-H, not K-diff → WH-H) and/or a VotingWeight-side smoothed/checkpointed tvl read; ALTERNATIVELY documented Accepted-risk given the quartic self-limiting + F-01/F-02 contest bounds. Full fork donation/swap sim (R1-blocked: pilots have no svZCHF path on the fork) → Stage-P sweep. RESOLVED (c283371 + 3d80ac8) — VotingWeight._positionPower now reads the 60-day `EMASampler.tvlEMA` (not spot `ORACLE.tvl`) behind an `EMA_MATURITY_BLOCKS = 60 × BLOCKS_PER_DAY` seed-age gate: a pool whose TVL EMA is unseeded (`emaSeedBlock == 0`) or younger than 60 days confers zero governance weight, closing both the atomic spot-pump and the F-D15 cold-start seed-to-spot window; a mature EMA still carries the F-9 quartic bound as defense-in-depth. The fix added `emaSeedBlock` first-seed exposure to EMASampler/IEMASampler (additive, Stage-F fix-forward, I13-class — no re-tag). The F04 PoC was flipped from proving the pump to proving the gate (`test_F04_FreshlySeededEmaPump_ConfersZeroWeight` + 3 more, 4/4 green). No substrate seam. | Fixed | `3d80ac8` (no substrate seam) | `test/whitehat/F04_spotTvlManipulation.t.sol` (4/4) |
| F-05 | 2026-06-14 | WK-R.5 | `src/governance/VotingWeight.sol` consuming `src/ccb/EMASampler.sol` | `_positionPower` (consumer) / `EMASampler.updateEMA` seed path | S7 | Low | Suspected | The F-04 EMA-maturity gate (`VotingWeight` L136-138) checks seed AGE (`block.number - emaSeedBlock >= 432_000`), not value freshness; `lastEMAUpdateBlock` is read by NEITHER consumer (`VotingWeight` grep 0; `CCBMultiplier` body grep 0 — only the L77 NatSpec; `IEMASampler` L16 "reserved for a future stale-EMA guard at Stage H", unimplemented). An inflated EMA — raw seed (`EMASampler` L138-141, smoothing-bypassed) or sustained multi-sample skew — confers quartically-bounded amplified governance weight once the 60-day AGE gate matures. | The seed path assigns `spotTVL` raw (donation / add-liquidity / swap-pumpable via `TVLOracle.tvl` L274-283 live `balancesLiveScaled18`); the consumer gates only on seed age (`VotingWeight` L138), so a stale inflated seed survives to maturity. Permissionless daily re-sampling is a weak mitigation: (59/61)^60 = 0.135, so 13.5% of an inflated seed survives 60 honest daily samples (half-life ~21d ≈ the 60d gate). Sustained-skew converges (steady state of (2·spot+59·old)/61 is EMA→spot; 60 daily pumped samples drive EMA 86.5% toward the pump). Neither variant is covered by the OQ-5a-bis single-block-spike acceptance (`EMASampler` L14-18): the seed path bypasses the cited smoothing, and multi-sample skew converges toward — not away from — the pumped value. Bounded LOW by the ENFORCED F-9 quartic root (`VotingWeight` L158 `ema.powDown(0.25e18)`: 100×→3.16×, 10000×→10× weight), the flash-immune recorded-share leg (OQ-25, L149), the 60-day real-capital commitment, relative-only advantage over other-pool holders (same-pool holders share the inflation), veto = blocking power not theft, and the opposing emission counter-incentive (`CCBMultiplier` L268 deltaIntra penalty on a high EMA). Cross-cutting: the freshness gap is present in both `tvlEMA` consumers; the emission consumer is self-correcting, the governance consumer is one-directional → governance-scoped. Disposition routed: CODE FIX = a `lastEMAUpdateBlock` freshness gate in `_positionPower` (and/or seed-path smoothing/anchor); ALTERNATIVELY documented Accepted-risk given the quartic self-limiting. RESOLVED (`d5cecb3` i + `70eb276` ii) — consumer-side freshness gate in `_positionPower`: `EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS = AureumTime.BLOCKS_PER_EPOCH` (14 days / one epoch) + `if (block.number - EMA_SAMPLER.lastEMAUpdateBlock(pool) > EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS) return 0;` after the seed-AGE maturity gate (`VotingWeight` L167) — `EMA_MATURITY_BLOCKS` guards seed AGE, this guards seed FRESHNESS, so a never-refreshed or pumped-then-abandoned seed (incl. the WK-R.6 100%-inflated pre-approval dormant-seed window) is structurally stale → 0 the instant the 60-day clock matures; `lastEMAUpdateBlock == 0` is fail-closed. Chosen over the seed-path smoothing/anchor alternative (minimal fail-closed surface, `EMASampler` byte-untouched). PoC `test/whitehat/F05_emaFreshnessGate.t.sol` (4/4): stale mature→0, fresh mature→weight, strict-`>` boundary pinned; 784 non-fork green. | F-06 | 2026-06-14 | WK-R.8 | `src/governance/AureumGovernance.sol` | `_voteSucceeded` / `state` / `queue` / `execute` (consuming `VotingWeight.totalSupply`) | S9 | Medium | Suspected | F-01's fix made the quorum denominator a LIVE `VOTING_WEIGHT.totalSupply()` read at tally (L294), but `forVotes`/`againstVotes` FREEZE at `endBlock` (castVote window L254) while `state`→`_voteSucceeded` re-reads the denominator live on every `queue`/`execute` (L276); an adversary holding DORMANT (un-poked) matured qualified LP pokes it into `_totalQualifiedWeight` AFTER `endBlock`, inflating the denominator past the frozen turnout's 20% bar → an otherwise-passing proposal griefed to Defeated/Expired. | Frozen-numerator-over-live-denominator asymmetry post-`endBlock`: `castVote` reverts `ProposalNotActive` once `block.number > endBlock` (L254) so `totalVotes` is final, but `_voteSucceeded` reads `VOTING_WEIGHT.totalSupply()` (= `_totalQualifiedWeight`, VotingWeight L97-99) live at every `state()` call (L276) and `poke` permissionlessly grows the accumulator at any block (VotingWeight L109-124). Attack: pre-position matured qualified LP, hold it DORMANT (never poked → absent from the accumulator), then post-`endBlock` poke it in so `totalVotes*10_000 < totalSupply()*2_000` (L294) → `_voteSucceeded` false → `state` short-circuits Defeated BEFORE the eta branches (L276 ahead of L277-279), so even a `queue`d proposal (eta set L315) reverts to Defeated and `execute` reverts `ProposalNotSucceeded` (L330); holding the inflation through `eta+EXECUTION_GRACE_BLOCKS` (L278) Expires it → a passed vote nullified (governance liveness/outcome integrity, no fund theft). Cost gating (→ Medium not High): the SAME real-matured-stake gate as F-02 — `poke` only adds weight for LP clearing `_positionPower`'s gauge-approval (VotingWeight L135) + 60-day-matured EMA (L138) + 14-day cliff (L142) + real recorder LP (L143-146); NOT a flash/spot attack. But the gate raises PRICE not closes the vector: (1) capital is RECOVERABLE (LP kept, unlike the burned proposal deposit L198-199) → opportunity-cost + gas, repeatable; (2) the strict `<` quorum (L294) kills a marginal (~20% turnout) proposal on an infinitesimal bump and a 25%-turnout one on ~25% accumulator inflation; (3) the natural adversary is the incumbent large LP of the challenged pool — exactly who holds dormant qualified stake, and the F-9 sublinear root `tvlEMA^e` (e=1/4 Era0 / 1/3 Era1+, L155-158) concentrates weight in few high-TVL pools where a whale dominates the addable denominator (pool-aggregate weight capped at `tvlEMA^e`, split among LPs by share). Symmetric weaker face — deflate-to-pass: withdraw + `poke` self down to lower the bar for a majority-but-sub-quorum proposal, floored by other holders' weight (cannot fabricate forVotes post-`endBlock`), milder. LINEAGE: NOT a regression — pre-F-01 the propose-time `snapshotTotalSupply` (L201/L204) was immutable so this vector did not exist; F-01's live read (correct for the >100%-turnout bug WK-R.2 re-proved) OPENS this inverse vector as a side effect, causally downstream of the fix, not an F-01 re-open. Confirms the WK-R.4 forward-ref (`state()` live-denominator block-via-pump → WK-R.8). Disposition routed (build-line, F-01 pattern): CODE FIX = snapshot the quorum DENOMINATOR at `endBlock` (pull-based lazy capture on the first post-`endBlock` `state()`, or an explicit `finalizeTally`), freezing numerator AND denominator at the tally boundary — reverting to propose-time `snapshotTotalSupply` reintroduces the F-01 bug, so the endBlock-time capture is the correct alignment; ALTERNATIVELY documented Accepted-risk given the real-stake gate + root-capped cost + ~16-day public contest. RESOLVED (97131f8 iii+iv + 427187a v, after 9b57d9c i + 8fdd476 ii) — Governor-style snapshot voting: a pre-vote `snapshotBlock` (`start + VOTING_DELAY_BLOCKS`) freezes BOTH the numerator (`getPastVotes(voter, snapshotBlock)` in `castVote` L260) and the denominator (`getPastTotalSupply(p.snapshotBlock)` in `_voteSucceeded` L300) at one OZ `Checkpoints.Trace208` block, so a post-`endBlock` `poke` moves only the live accumulator, never the snapshotBlock checkpoint the tally reads — closing the inflation grief AND the symmetric deflate-to-pass face and converting F-01's live read into a structural numerator≤denominator invariant; SUPERSEDES the e6d9b3d live-read denominator (F-01) and the WK-R.8 endBlock-only sketch (`ProposalState.Pending` appended, `poke` dropped from `castVote`). PoC `test/whitehat/F06_postEndBlockDenominatorFreeze.t.sol` (block-aware mock, 2/2) proves both faces; F01 suite re-pointed to the snapshot model (5/5); 780 non-fork green. | F-07 | 2026-06-17 | WL.1 | `src/incendiary/IncendiaryRegistry.sol` | `_maturePrice` (consumed by `_valueInAuMM` / `buyBoost`) | S7 | Low | Confirmed | `_maturePrice` (L332-339) enforced only the F-04 seed-AGE maturity gate (`seedBlock==0`→EMANotMature L334; `age < EMA_MATURITY_BLOCKS`→EMANotMature L336) and NOT sample-FRESHNESS, so a mature-but-stale der-Bodensee price-EMA rail (seasoned ≥60d but `lastSampleBlock` far behind `block.number`) priced every `buyBoost` deposit→entitlement valuation off a stale stored EMA indefinitely — the IncendiaryRegistry sibling of the F-05 `VotingWeight` freshness gap. | `_maturePrice` never read `block.number - rail.lastSampleBlock`; `lastSampleBlock` advances only on permissionless `updateRailEMA` (L317, cadence ≥ BLOCKS_PER_DAY). Once a rail's seed seasons the 60-day `EMA_MATURITY_BLOCKS`, an inflated-or-merely-stale EMA priced `buyBoost` (L268) via `_valueInAuMM` (L411), BEFORE the deposit `safeTransferFrom` payment pull, at a stale rate until someone re-sampled. Unlike F-05's quartically-damped governance weight, the boost valuation is rate-scaled-LINEAR off the EMA, so a stale EMA maps deposit value to entitlement at the stale price directly. Bounded LOW by the 5% anti-gaming haircut, the F-04 maturity gate (no fresh-seed pump), the L-D6 15%-of-epoch aggregate boost cap (per-epoch over-entitlement ceiling), the real svZCHF/sUSDS deposit cost donated to der Bodensee (L-D2 — buyer pays real value), relative-only mispricing (vs current spot, not theft), and permissionless re-sampling. Fix: consumer-side freshness gate mirroring F-05 — `EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS = AureumTime.BLOCKS_PER_EPOCH` (14d / one epoch, L47) + `if (block.number - rail.lastSampleBlock > EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS) revert EMAStale(payToken, staleness, EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS)` (L337-338) after the maturity gate, fail-closed (a never-refreshed seed is structurally stale the instant the 60-day clock matures). Test retrofit (F-07.2) added a fresh re-sample before each pre-existing mature-rail buyBoost/extMaturePrice test + a `test_buyBoost_revert_emaStale` negative. | F-13 | 2026-07-01 | WH-D.2 | `src/fee_router/AureumFeeRoutingHook.sol` | `_swapExactInFeeTokenToSvZchfViaVault` (L487-504) + `_addLiquidityOneSidedToBodenseeViaVault` (L530-563) | S5 | Low | Confirmed | The hook's two internal fee-conversion legs set `limitRaw: 0` (L494) and `minBptAmountOut: 0` (L552) — no slippage bound — so the per-swap protocol fee routed inside `onAfterSwap` (fee-token → svZCHF → one-sided Bodensee add) is sandwichable: a searcher front-runs to skew `params.pool`, the hook accepts any output, Bodensee receives less svZCHF than fair, the back-run captures the spread. | Value leak from the protocol fee stream to MEV; attacker ROI > 0 in principle. Bounded LOW: each event routes a dust-sized amount (≈ 50% × the pool swap fee on a single trade, per token), so realistic extraction is gas-bounded, though systematic across all swaps. The conversion is settlement-embedded (atomic with the user's swap), so the practical form is a sandwich of the victim's trade where the unprotected fee-rider leaks slightly beyond the victim's own slippage protection. Acknowledged in-code (`AureumFeeRoutingHook.sol` L514-517) and pre-flagged as a Stage-Q surface (`STAGE_D_PLAN.md:L703`); the hook holds no oracle to derive a bound. Disposition (user-adjudicated 2026-07-01): LOG as Accepted-risk, deferred to Stage Q — candidate mitigation is a rate-derived `limitRaw`/`minBptAmountOut` bound, or accept the dust-bound rationale. Not a new discovery — an accepted design trade-off logged for coverage completeness + the Stage-Q tier-1 audit and a quantifying sandwich sim. SPLIT re-adjudication (PB-D9, user-adjudicated 2026-07-13): scope re-ground at HEAD — the WH-D pointers predate F-14 / PB2.1, and the shared `_swapFeeAndDeposit` primitive now has four consumers, not one. (a) onAfterSwap (the per-swap path this row was filed against) — ACCEPTED, 0/0 bounds retained: bounding rejected on circularity (spot-derived bounds move with the same-bundle skew; no lagged oracle for arbitrary fee tokens on arbitrary pools), hot-path gas, and an F-14-class liveness regression (a bound tripping inside settlement-embedded `onAfterSwap` reverts the user's swap); quantified on the mainnet fork (PB2.4d2) — `feeRider = 0.1875e18` (0.375% of a 50e18 swap) and the skew round-trip costs 12× the entire fee-rider prize, fee-negative at every realistic size; severity stays S5 Low on this half. (b) routeYieldFee / routeYieldFeeToHook (the PB2.1 controller entry, added post-audit — the 2026-07-01 dust rationale never covered it) — FIXED: routes an epoch (~14 days) of accumulated yield fees in one public-mempool call, non-dust once governance sets the yield fee; caller-supplied `minDepositTokenOut` + `minBptAmountOut` now enforce natively via the Vault (`SwapLimit` / `BptAmountOutBelowMin`), a tripped bound reverting only the route call (PB-D6 stamp-after-success leaves the epoch unburned). (c) routeGovernanceDeposit / routeIncendiaryDeposit — DORMANT (zero callers; Stage K / L route via `SwapAndDepositToBodensee.donate`, module setters unset by design), threaded with the same two params as future-safe interface hygiene, attested not a wiring commitment. F-23 amendment (PB-D68 (xiv), 2026-08-08): the `minBptAmountOut` half of the (b) fix is RETIRED by F-23 rather than weakened by oversight. The shared phase-2 helper now donates instead of minting, so `AddLiquidityKind.DONATION` returns `bptOut == 0` by construction and no floor above zero is satisfiable at any value; the hook rejects a nonzero one outright with `BptFloorUnavailableOnDonation` rather than forwarding it to the Vault's `BptAmountOutBelowMin`. The parameter survives on all three external entries for ABI stability per PB-D68 (vi) and is now a checked constant. What this costs F-13 is scope, not strength: the phase-2 add can no longer be sandwiched for BPT because no BPT is minted at all, and the sandwich surface PB-D9 quantified was always the phase-1 swap leg, which `minDepositTokenOut` still bounds unchanged. Disposition (a) onAfterSwap is UNAFFECTED and stays Accepted-risk at S5 Low on the PB2.4d2 evidence. The four bounded-route witnesses in the PoC column move accordingly: `test_Fork_RouteYieldFee_minBptAmountOut_revertsWhenTooTight` now expects `BptFloorUnavailableOnDonation` in place of `BptAmountOutBelowMin`, `test_Fork_RouteYieldFee_addLegSucceedsAtRealisticBound` keeps its name as historical while testing the zero floor that is now the only satisfiable one, and `test_Fork_RouteYieldFee_minDepositTokenOut_revertsWhenTooTight` plus `test_Fork_RouteYieldFee_swapLegSucceedsAtRealisticBound` are untouched, the former being the surviving positive witness that F-13's protection still enforces. | SPLIT — onAfterSwap Accepted-risk (S5 Low) / routeYieldFee Fixed / dormant entries hardened + attested dormant | SPLIT (PB-D9, `6fc8416`): onAfterSwap 0/0 retained (dust; accepted); routeYieldFee + dormant entries bounded via caller-supplied minDepositTokenOut + minBptAmountOut → Vault-native SwapLimit / BptAmountOutBelowMin — `ebf5aaa` (src: interface + impl + controller pass-through) + `69e77c9` + `1e91e2f` (unit) + `6fc1625` (bounded-route fork witness) + `137f1bd` (onAfterSwap sandwich sim) (no substrate seam) | `test/fork/AureumFeeRoutingHook.t.sol` — bounded-route witness (`test_Fork_RouteYieldFee_minDepositTokenOut_revertsWhenTooTight` + `test_Fork_RouteYieldFee_minBptAmountOut_revertsWhenTooTight` + `test_Fork_RouteYieldFee_swapLegSucceedsAtRealisticBound` + `test_Fork_RouteYieldFee_addLegSucceedsAtRealisticBound`, PB2.4d1) + onAfterSwap sandwich sim (`test_Fork_F13_feeRiderPerSwapIsDust` + `test_Fork_F13_skewCostExceedsFeeRiderPrize`, PB2.4d2; feeRider 0.375% of swap, skewCost 12× the prize) + unit bound coverage in `test/unit/AureumFeeRoutingHook.t.sol` + `test/unit/AureumProtocolFeeController.t.sol` (PB2.4c1/c2); 14/14 fork + 901 unit |

Original revert PoC `fa1dbbd`. VENUE WALL: a per-fee-token conversion-venue registry is infeasible — 8 of the 12 distinct tokens across the two pools have no svZCHF-liquid venue anywhere in the constellation (scrvUSD/USDC/PYUSD/USDT; sfrxETH/rETH/weETH/wOETH), and the 4 ixAetheron LSTs appear ONLY in ixAetheron (no first hop for a bridge). Alternatives dropped: the svZCHF config-fix (CHF/ETH FX cost breaking the N-D8 native-4626 identity; svZCHF is admitted-4626 so it would have RAISED the QG — dropped on product grounds) and an on-chain ixEDEL 2-hop bridge (multi-hop + bridge registry + cross-pool coupling + doubled F-13 slippage on the crown-jewel hot path for one pool). F-13's unbounded-internal-leg slippage class unchanged (sUSDS legs inherit it → Stage Q). LINEAGE: the F-06 sibling — F-06 fixed the identical live-denominator gaming in AureumGovernance's quorum via a snapshotBlock checkpoint, but VaultClassRegistry's veto path was not migrated. | F-21 | 2026-08-05 | PB3.8p | `src/governance/AureumGovernance.sol` | `_voteSucceeded` (consumed by `state` → `queue` / `execute`) | S8/S9 | High | Confirmed | A zero `getPastTotalSupply(p.snapshotBlock)` makes the quorum test vacuous — `0 * 10_000 < 0 * QUORUM_BPS` is false — and the `CompositionChallenge` branch's non-strict `forVotes * 3 >= totalVotes * 2` then returns true at `0 >= 0`, so a composition challenge Succeeds with NO vote cast by anyone and captures a Miliarium slot through queue and execute, during a launch window in which no holder is able to vote at all. | The strictness divergence between branches is the symptom; the defect is the vacuous denominator. Gauge and Fee return `forVotes > againstVotes`, false at `0 > 0`, and are therefore safe only ACCIDENTALLY — their safety rests on `VotingWeight` L24's invariant that `_totalQualifiedWeight` equals the sum of `_holderWeight[*]`, a property of a different contract, rather than on any check local to the tally. Reachability is unconditional rather than opportunistic: `VotingWeight._positionPower` L163-L172 short-circuits to zero unless the pool's TVL EMA has been seeding for `EMA_MATURITY_BLOCKS`, 432,000 blocks or 60 days, so before that boundary every holder in every pool reads zero power, `poke` computes a `newWeight` equal to its `oldWeight` of zero, takes the no-op short-circuit at L143 and never pushes a checkpoint — `_totalQualifiedWeightHistory` stays EMPTY and `getPastTotalSupply` returns zero for every block in the window. That is not a state an operator can fall into or avoid; it is the launch condition of every chain the protocol is deployed to. No defense exists inside it, which is the sharpest departure from F-01's disposition: the same invariant forces every `getPastVotes(holder, snapshotBlock)` to zero whenever the total is zero, so no honest party can cast an against-vote at ANY weight, and F-01's bounding observation that one larger honest against-vote defeats the attacker cannot fire — the single condition that vacates the denominator also empties every ballot that might oppose it. F-06 froze the denominator as a historical read, so no post-snapshot `poke` moves it either. Cost and fit: `GaugeEligibility.meetsCompositionQualityGate` L385-L392 is a STATIC structural check (fee-routing hook identity, approved-factory provenance, 52 percent weight numerator clearing `0.52e18`) with no TVL floor, no age requirement and no EMA dependency, so a pool deployed on day 0 passes it on day 0 and the gate is not the cost barrier F-21's PoC scope left open; `proposeCompositionChallenge` requires NO voting weight, only the non-refundable 1,000 svZCHF deposit; `VOTING_DELAY_BLOCKS` 7,200 plus `VOTING_PERIOD_BLOCKS` 100,800 plus `EXECUTION_TIMELOCK_BLOCKS` 14,400 is 122,400 blocks, roughly 17 days, fitting inside the 432,000-block window three times over; `queue` and `execute` are both permissionless so the attacker drives the whole sequence alone; and nothing serialises proposals, so all 26 filled slots are available in parallel for 26,000 svZCHF. There is no administrative remedy: `script/DeployStageK.s.sol` L159 calls `setGovernanceContract(address(governance))` on `MILIARIUM_REGISTRY` — which is how the composition path reaches `replaceSlot` at all — as part of the Stage F through K stack already broadcast to Sepolia in phase 4, not a dissolution-time rotation, and `MiliariumRegistry` is deliberately absent from PB2.6's five-rotation roster; since `replaceSlot` and `setGovernanceContract` are BOTH `onlyGovernance` there, once the bind lands the multisig can neither re-slot directly nor rotate the role back. That bounds permanence without restoring any defense — the honest side can counter-propose on identical terms for its own deposit and its own 17 days, so inside the window slot occupancy belongs to whoever proposed most recently while the captured slot earns emissions throughout each cycle. Why latent through every prior pass: F-06 KNEW about this condition and recorded it as an explicit open carry, `test_F06_vacuousQuorumAtZeroSnapshotStillOpen`, whose own NatSpec said it was asserted so a future fix would flip it — but it was only ever exercised on the Gauge branch, where `0 > 0` defeats and a 1e18 vote was needed to reach Succeeded. Nobody ran the same zero denominator through the Composition branch, where `0 >= 0` passes with no vote at all. The escalation from carried-observation to High is the BRANCH, not the condition. Severity High rather than Medium because the defense is structurally unavailable rather than merely requiring vigilance and because no governance weight whatsoever is needed to propose, where F-01 at least required a single qualified holder; High rather than Critical because LP principal and the Vault substrate are untouched, no funds are stolen, the sequence is loud on chain and takes 17 days, and restoration is symmetric. Disposition (user-adjudicated 2026-08-05, PB-D62): FIX at the denominator, not at the branch — `_voteSucceeded` reads the snapshot supply once into a local `snapshotSupply` and returns false when it is zero, before either majority branch is reached. The guard closes both branches at once, makes Gauge and Fee safety LOCAL to the tally instead of derived from a cross-contract invariant, and covers a re-entry path no launch sequencing could reach, in which an attacker permissionlessly pokes every holder to zero under a protocol-wide EMA staleness and re-opens the window mid-life; that mid-life path is reasoned from `poke`'s permissionlessness and the `EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS` short-circuit and is recorded as an OPEN ASSESSMENT rather than a demonstrated vector, not PoC'd. Accepted-risk was considered and declined, since an operational mitigation delaying the Stage K registry bind past EMA maturity would close the launch window but leave the mid-life path open. The belt-and-braces alternative of also rejecting a zero `totalVotes` was considered and declined as redundant: a non-zero supply forces `totalVotes` above the 20 percent quorum floor before either majority branch is evaluated, leaving the `>=` form sound as written. Blast radius sized at pre-flight (I13): `test/unit/AureumGovernance.t.sol` is unaffected because its `setUp` sets a non-zero supply at L157; the two mocks hardcoding `getPastTotalSupply` to zero (`test/fork/mocks/StageGMocks.sol` L57, `test/unit/VaultClassRegistry.t.sol` L52) feed other consumers and appear in no AureumGovernance tally path; `F06_postEndBlockDenominatorFreeze.t.sol` uses a block-aware mock returning 1,000,000e18; the derivation of a NON-EXISTENT proposal stays `Defeated` because `GaugeChallenge` is the zero value of `ProposalType` and the zero proposal therefore takes the strict branch. The sizing initially missed one file-local case and was corrected before drafting: the F-06 carry test inverts too, so THREE tests in the PoC file changed, not two. Vault substrate untouched — no bytecode seam. Sibling lineage: F-01 (the propose-time denominator this supersedes), F-06 (the snapshot freeze that closed F-01 and carried this case forward), F-12 (the composition quality gate that is not the barrier it was assumed to be). | Fixed | `1fc0c66` (the `_voteSucceeded` zero-supply guard; `snapshotSupply` cached to a single `getPastTotalSupply` read, `return false` rather than a revert so `state()` stays total over non-existent proposals) + `ccccc19` (PoC inverted to regression, the stale F-06 carry test replaced, contract NatSpec corrected) + PB-D62 design lock `50cf99d` + Decisions row `5085947`. No substrate seam. | `test/whitehat/F01_quorumSnapshotTiming.t.sol` (7/7 — `test_F21_compositionZeroVoteCannotCaptureSlotAtZeroSnapshot` drives the capture path and asserts `Defeated`, `queue` reverting `ProposalNotSucceeded`, and the slot plus both gauge flags unmoved, with the premise asserted BEFORE the attempt so an unchanged slot is the guard holding rather than the fixture having started there; `test_F21_zeroSnapshotDefeatsGaugeDespiteVotes` closes the F-06 carry at a real 1e18 turnout; `test_F21_gaugeChallengeZeroVoteDefeatsAtSameSnapshot` retained as the Gauge-side companion, no longer a discriminator now that both branches agree). Pre-fix capture PoC at `0b97d3c`, which drove the same sequence THROUGH queue and execute to slot capture. Part-A gate 961/961 green, 57 suites, 0 failed. |
| F-22 | 2026-08-05 | PB3.6b | `src/governance/AureumGovernanceAuthorizer.sol` (layer one) + `src/governance/AureumGovernance.sol` (layer two) | `_isEmergencyAction` L75-L77 and `canPerform` L65-L73 on the authorizer; `execute` / `_executeProposal` L343-L374 and the `Proposal` struct on governance; the stranded surface is `VaultAdmin.sol`'s eleven `authenticate`-gated entries, cited READ-ONLY | S8 | Critical | Confirmed | `AureumGovernanceAuthorizer.canPerform` grants `GOVERNANCE_CONTRACT` every actionId unconditionally, but `AureumGovernance.execute()` dispatches over a fixed proposal-type enumeration whose `Proposal` struct carries no target field and no calldata field, so nine of the Vault's eleven `authenticate`-gated admin entries are permitted to governance and reachable by no caller at all — and the two survivors, `pauseVault` and `enableRecoveryMode`, are both ENTRIES whose inverses sit among the nine, making every emergency lever a door that opens only inward. | The PoC separates permission from expressibility across three fork-green faces: the premise face asserts `canPerform` L65-L73 returns true for `GOVERNANCE_CONTRACT` on an arbitrary actionId and on the zero actionId alike, without exception; the dispositive face pranks the governance contract as `msg.sender` and watches the Vault ACCEPT `setProtocolFeeController`, mutating state that no on-chain transaction could ever produce; the third face pranks the in-fork multisig and takes `SenderNotAllowed`. The defect is therefore not a missing grant — the grant is total, and what is missing is any code path able to emit the call. `VaultAdmin.sol` carries eleven `authenticate`-gated entries (`pauseVault`, `unpauseVault`, `setProtocolFeeController`, `enableRecoveryMode`, `disableRecoveryMode`, `disableQuery`, `disableQueryPermanently`, `enableQuery`, `pauseVaultBuffers`, `unpauseVaultBuffers`, `setAuthorizer`); nine are reachable by no caller at all, and the two exceptions are reachable only by `EMERGENCY_MULTISIG` and only while `block.number < EMERGENCY_WINDOW_END_BLOCK`, because `_isEmergencyAction` L75-L77 admits exactly those two actionIds. The two entered states differ in stickiness and the worse one compounds with a permissionless step: a Vault pause self-clears at `_vaultBufferPeriodEndTime` (`VaultCommon._isVaultPaused` L149-L152), so with the Stage-A row-7 four-year window plus six-month buffer an unpausable pause lifts at roughly deploy plus four and a half years, while recovery mode does not self-clear and is therefore permanent; and `enableRecoveryMode` L346-L356 skips `_authenticateCaller()` entirely once the pool or the Vault is already paused, so after any pause ANY anonymous address may place ANY pool into permanent recovery mode. The pause precondition does not even require the emergency window — pool `pauseManager` is the governance multisig and `_ensureAuthenticatedByRole` L787-L793 is NON-exclusive, so the multisig may pause individual Miliarium pools through the role at any time. The window bounds entry but not the state entered, which defeats naive symmetry: a pause or a recovery-mode entry on the last block of the window outlives it, so any exit authority must either outlive the entry authority or be backed by a second permanent path. Severity Critical, recorded with its reasoning so the call does not drift: the distinguishing property is that the trigger is CORRECT USE rather than misuse — F-21 required a deliberate attacker and a non-refundable 1,000 svZCHF deposit, whereas this requires only that an incident occur and that a responder do exactly what the mechanism exists for. Weighed against Critical: no funds are stolen and pools in recovery mode still permit proportional exit, so custody is intact and the loss is the protocol's function rather than its users' balances. Weighed for: the outcome is permanent, protocol-wide, reachable without an adversary, and unreversible by every party including the one that caused it. Why latent through every prior pass: that `AureumGovernance` v1 exposes no generic-call hatch is already on record at K-D9, at PB-D12 (ii) which triages the stranded Aureum-owned setters, and at PB-D61 / RB-017 for the hook's `governanceModule` — but every one of those concerns a contract gated by its own `governanceContract` slot, and no document enumerated the VAULT's own admin surface, reached instead through the authorizer, which the identical fixed dispatch strands identically. Three live artifacts presupposed the opposite and were wrong post-K; all three are corrected in the fix. Disposition (user-adjudicated 2026-08-05, PB-D63 + PB-D64): FIX in source landing for the Stage-R mainnet deploy, with Sepolia NOT remediated by decision rather than omission — the deployed contracts are immutable, the finding is exactly what a testnet exists to surface, and this is the disposition shape PB-D52 (viii) already set when it declined the ten-contract cascade and left the live oracle pre-fix. The fix is TWO-LAYERED because a proposal can never be an emergency response: `VOTING_DELAY_BLOCKS` 7,200 plus `VOTING_PERIOD_BLOCKS` 100,800 plus `EXECUTION_TIMELOCK_BLOCKS` 14,400 is 122,400 blocks, about seventeen days, which is acceptable for LEAVING an emergency state and unacceptable for entering one. Layer one widens `_isEmergencyAction` to four actionIds, pairing each entry with its inverse, with the exit grant expiring on the SAME window as entry per PB-D63 (x) — the most §xxix-conservative reading, and load-bearing in one direction only: die-with-entry MUST NOT be adopted without layer two, or a month-eleven recovery mode becomes permanent exactly as it is today. Layer two adds three constrained proposal types, `VaultAuthorizerChange` / `VaultUnpause` / `VaultRecoveryDisable`, with named targets, no calldata field and no generic dispatch, amending PB-D63 (ix)'s one-type wording per PB-D64 (i); the rejected alternative of one type carrying a sub-enum fails on two counts, a second dispatch level inside a single `_executeProposal` branch where the contract's existing shape is one type to one path with no nesting anywhere, and a forced `_voteSucceeded` branch on the sub-type the moment the majorities differ, which they do. `setAuthorizer` is the keystone and is ranked first because every `authenticate` call resolves through `_authorizer.canPerform`, so leaving it unreachable would make every permission decision taken at Stage R correct-or-stranded on first deploy, forever; but restoring it ALONE is insufficient in a circular way worth writing down — governance installs a new authorizer, that authorizer grants some principal, and the principal must still be able to EXPRESS the call, so granting to `AureumGovernance` reproduces F-22 exactly while granting to a fresh contract able to make arbitrary Vault calls is a generic hatch under another name, owed its own threat model. The typed-domain shape (PB-D64): the enum values APPEND, because enum values are positional and stored proposals plus every external decoder of `getProposal` key on the index; `Proposal` gains exactly one member, `address newAuthorizer`, read only by `VaultAuthorizerChange`, while `VaultRecoveryDisable` reuses the existing `targetPool` and `VaultUnpause` carries no payload at all, taking the struct from thirteen members to fourteen — additive in storage, and a tuple-width change for any external decoder of `getProposal`, which is the one caller-visible consequence and is named here so it is not discovered later. Majorities differ BY DIRECTION, deliberately: `VaultAuthorizerChange` joins the existing `CompositionChallenge` two-thirds branch because it is the lever that redefines every permission in the system and should not pass on a lower bar than swapping a single Miliarium slot, while `VaultUnpause` and `VaultRecoveryDisable` take the simple-majority branch because they are de-escalation and it would be perverse to make leaving an emergency state harder than the bar that never guarded entering one. The shared two-day timelock is explicitly considered and retained rather than split per type. `proposeVaultAuthorizerChange` carries propose-time guards only — the existing `ZeroAddress()` plus a new `AuthorizerNotContract(address newAuthorizer)` for a codeless address, since an authorizer that is either would make every `authenticate` call in the Vault revert permanently, a failure strictly worse than the one F-22 documents and reached by a passing vote rather than by an attack; propose-time suffices by the F-20 precedent, because the value is frozen in the proposal at creation and no mutable eligibility surface sits between propose and execute. The other two entries carry NO propose-time guard, since `disableRecoveryMode`'s own `withRegisteredPool` and `_ensurePoolInRecoveryMode` checks fail loud and predictably at execute time with no F-20-style state that can look valid at propose time and silently break later. One implementation hazard was structural rather than incidental: `_executeProposal`'s trailing bare `else` was silently bound to `FeeChange` — the exact fallthrough shape F-22 itself diagnoses — so it converts to an explicit else-if chain with six named branches and no trailing catch-all, without which all three new types would have fallen into the fee-change path and called `setStaticSwapFeePercentage` on a zero pool. Blast radius carried one named trap (PB-D64 (vii)): `test_canPerform_emergencyMultisigNonEmergencyAction_false` is a fuzz over `bytes32` that `vm.assume`s away exactly the then-current emergency actionIds, so widening the set would NOT have failed it — the odds of the fuzzer producing a specific `bytes32` are negligible — it would simply have stopped asserting what its name claims; the assume set grew to four in the same commit. ACCEPTED RESIDUAL, and the structural difference from F-21: the F-22 PoC does NOT invert. `setProtocolFeeController` and the other non-exit entries stay unreachable after the full fix, because PB-D63 (xi) scopes F-22 to the exits plus the keystone and nothing more, so the PoC stays green and changes role from proof-of-defect to attestation-of-accepted-residual. The residual is closable later without reopening this decision: `setAuthorizer` reachability plus a principal able to express arbitrary Vault calls is precisely the CircuitBreaker track PB-D63 (xi) split into its own line so it cannot gate Stage R. Vault substrate cited READ-ONLY throughout (`VaultAdmin.sol` L346-L356 / L787-L793, `VaultCommon.sol` L149-L152) — Vault bytecode untouched, no substrate seam. Sibling lineage: F-20 (the other Vault-facing governance-liveness finding, where an exclusive role rather than a fixed dispatch was the foreclosing mechanism), F-21 (the immediately preceding governance finding, and the severity anchor this row is calibrated against), PB-D61 / RB-017 (the same fixed-dispatch strand on the hook's `governanceModule`), K-D9 (the proposal-type triad this extends to six). | Fixed | `5bef424` (rung a, layer one — `_isEmergencyAction` widened from two actionIds to four, pairing each entry with its inverse; the authorizer unit suite 12 to 14 tests, with the fuzz-exclusion assume set repaired to all four) + `4e15d3c` (rung b1, layer-two declarations — the three appended `ProposalType` values, the `IAuthorizer` import, the `AuthorizerNotContract` error, header NatSpec corrected from three proposal types to six) + `a8cfa8b` (rung b2/b3, layer-two expressibility — the fourteenth `Proposal` member `newAuthorizer`, the seventh `_createProposal` parameter with `address(0)` at the three existing call sites, the `VaultAuthorizerChange` join to the two-thirds branch, the trailing bare `else` converted to an explicit six-branch else-if chain with no catch-all, and the three `propose*` entries) + `0c6024c` (rung d, the three stale-artifact corrections PB-D63 (vii) names, in `docs/STAGE_A_PLAN.md` row 4, `src/vault/AureumVaultFactory.sol` L28 and the `test/fork/DissolutionGovernanceHandoff.t.sol` pause attestation; comments and prose only, no behavioral change) + PB-D63 design lock `532ec4b` + PB-D64 build-shape lock `657739b`. No substrate seam. | `test/fork/F22_vaultAdminSurfaceUnreachable.t.sol` (3/3, added pre-fix at `c060c6a` and retained GREEN post-fix — the one PoC in this ledger that does not invert, reframed at `957ec73` from proof-of-defect to attestation-of-accepted-residual per PB-D64 (viii), its header NatSpec corrected there from three proposal types to six and from two emergency actions to four so it does not read as a stale unclosed failure). Reachability evidence at `957ec73`: twelve new `test/unit/AureumGovernance.t.sol` cases — five propose-side tests asserting payload placement (`newAuthorizer` set and zero, `targetPool` reused for `VaultRecoveryDisable`, `VaultUnpause` carrying no payload) plus the two `AuthorizerNotContract` / `ZeroAddress` propose-time guards, each pinning `donateCalls` at 0 to prove the guard fires BEFORE the deposit is pulled; four majority-boundary tests exercising PB-D64 (iv)'s asymmetry directly, the same 200k/100k and 175k/125k splits that pass and defeat `CompositionChallenge`'s two-thirds bar reproducing identically for `VaultAuthorizerChange` while `VaultUnpause` and `VaultRecoveryDisable` pass at a simple-majority split; and three execute-side tests confirming each type's dispatch reaches its named `MockVault` call exactly once. Against the real mainnet-fork Vault, `test/fork/StagePIntegration.t.sol::test_F22_authorizerChangeExecutesEndToEnd` drives the keystone `setAuthorizer` through a genuine propose/vote/queue/execute cycle, deliberately scoped to `setAuthorizer` alone since `VaultUnpause` and `VaultRecoveryDisable` share identical plumbing already proven at the unit level. | F-23 | 2026-08-07 | PB3.11a | `src/fee_router/AureumFeeRoutingHook.sol` | `_addLiquidityOneSidedToBodenseeViaVault`, the shared phase-2 helper reached by all four routing rails through `_swapFeeAndDeposit`: `onAfterSwap`, `routeYieldFee`, `routeGovernanceDeposit`, `routeIncendiaryDeposit` | S5 | High | Confirmed | The helper deposited protocol fees into der Bodensee with `AddLiquidityKind.UNBALANCED`, minting BPT on every fee route — a redeemable claim on depth the canonical spec requires to be irreversible and says so twice, "No wei of AuMM, svZCHF, or sUSDS is transferred out" and der Bodensee framed as "every swap fee deepens the lake" with no buyback, burn or market-purchase logic anywhere in the protocol. Der Bodensee was created with `enableDonation = true`, so DONATION was available to the hook from the first block, and the two sibling subsystems donating the same value, `BodenseeBootstrapChannel._distributeCallback` and `SwapAndDepositToBodensee`, both already revert `BptMintedOnDonation` on a single wei. | AuMM still reprices correctly under either mechanism, since weighted-pool spot price is a function of rate-scaled balances and weights rather than of BPT supply, so that half of the spec's mechanism was never at risk; what differs is ownership and reversibility. A donation raises every existing LP's claim on a deeper pool, whereas an UNBALANCED add mints new BPT that dilutes them proportionally and hands the increment to the recipient as a claim on exactly the depth just added. Recipients differ per rail and the lock was corrected on this at PB-D68 (xiii): `onAfterSwap` minted to the hook itself, while the three `IAureumFeeRoutingHook` entries minted to `caller`, so yield-fee BPT went to `AureumProtocolFeeController` and the two dormant rails to the governance and incendiary modules — which narrows the unexercisability argument, since for three rails the holder is a different contract with its own paths. Exposure was nil and measured rather than assumed: der Bodensee's BPT `totalSupply` reads 0 on Sepolia and the hook's BPT balance reads 0, so no add of any kind ever completed, and Sepolia cannot exercise the yield leg at all because `StubERC4626.convertToAssets` is `pure`. Provenance per PB-D68 (iii): the question was never decided wrongly, it was never put — Stage D debated Router versus direct Vault call and nothing else, and `kind: UNBALANCED` arrived inherited from the original plan's `IRouter.addLiquiditySingleTokenExactIn`, which mints BPT by construction. The fix moves the shared helper to `AddLiquidityKind.DONATION` with three guards: `BptMintedOnDonation` rejects any nonzero `bptOut`, `ReserveDidNotRise` requires der Bodensee's reserve to rise, and `BptFloorUnavailableOnDonation` rejects a nonzero `minBptAmountOut`, which is unsatisfiable under DONATION and leaves F-13's bounded-route protection resting on `minDepositTokenOut` alone; the vestigial `bptRecipient` and the `caller` parameter feeding it are both retired. Two corrections were needed during the fix and both are recorded rather than buried: PB-D68 (xvii) withdrew an exact reserve-delta assertion after eleven fork failures showed the Vault's DONATION branch round-trips raw through scaled18 where UNBALANCED deep-copies `maxAmountsIn`, and (xix) withdrew a settle-before-add inversion that over-credited the unlock and tripped `BalanceNotSettled`. G-D11's clause sanctioning an UNBALANCED fee path alongside DONATION is SUPERSEDED per PB-D68 (vii). lives — it records that a surface was checked and came back clean, so silence is
never mistaken for absence of review. Surfaces are `S0`–`S12`; mark each
`✓` (checked, clean), a finding ID (e.g. `F-07`), `n/a` (contract doesn't expose
it), or `—` (deferred — explain in Notes).

**WK.1 · S1 supply/mint integrity · `src/token/AuMMMinterRouter.sol` — NO FINDING.**
- WK.1 S1: NO FINDING | checked: single mint path (`mintFor` L96-100); cap-not-circumvented (AuMM `mint` L114 ceiling runs on every forward; boundary-tested at cap / cap+1, test L75-87); INV-1 cumulative ceiling (no-burn confirmed AuMM L15); INV-6 two-layer minter gate (AuMM L113 + router L97); reentrancy (router has zero mutable state, AuMM has no transfer hooks); no era/overflow surface in router | could-not-assess: upstream `amount` computation (→WK.3 distributor, WK.5 channel); deploy-time `setMinter` handoff (→WK.17).
- Observation — the cap is an "ever-minted" ceiling only because AuMM has no burn path (no ERC20Burnable, AuMM L15); re-derive INV-1 if any future stage adds burn to AuMM.
- Observation — `mintFor` does not guard `recipient != address(0)`; relies on OZ `_mint` zero-receiver revert downstream (defense-in-depth, not a defect).
- Harness — `test/unit/AuMMMinterRouter.t.sol` 10/10 green.
**WK.2 · S8 access control & privilege · `src/token/AuMMMinterRouter.sol` — NO FINDING.**
- WK.2 S8: NO FINDING | checked: allowlist bypass (exact `msg.sender` equality on two immutables L97 — no `tx.origin`, no signature/permit); immutability (`AUMM`/`BOOTSTRAP_CHANNEL`/`EMISSION_DISTRIBUTOR` `immutable` L27/36/45, ctor-set + zero-checked L77-83, assigned nowhere else in repo); no escalation surface (`mintFor` is the sole function — no fallback/receive/delegatecall/selfdestruct/assembly/owner/admin/setter); governance cannot widen the allowlist or re-point mint (no governance-callable surface); permanent-minter lock (AuMM `_minterAdmin` zeroed L104, router has no re-delegation) | could-not-assess: deploy-baked consumer-address correctness (→WK.17 / S10); consumer-side `setMintRouter` binding (→WK.4 / WK.6).
- Observation (design property, accepted per locked architecture CLAUDE.md §1/§2) — the router is an irrevocable minter with no admin / pause / upgrade / kill-switch, consistent with the no-owner fair-launch design. Consequence: no runtime backstop halts emission if a consumer is later found exploitable, so consumer correctness (WK.3–WK.6) is load-bearing because nothing can revoke the router post-deploy. Not a finding; conscious risk-acceptance.
- Observation (scope boundary) — the router does not constrain `recipient`; recipient authority is fully delegated to the two consumers. Router S8 cleanliness does not extend to recipient-integrity (→WK.4 / WK.6).
**WK.3 · S1 supply/mint integrity · `src/emission/EmissionDistributor.sol` (`claim` mint path via router) — NO FINDING (K-diff surface).**
- WK.3 S1: NO FINDING (K-diff surface) | checked: sole mint site `claim` L535 `mintRouter.mintFor` → router → AuMM cap L114 (router-wiring faithful, cap preserved); CEI effects L532-533 precede interaction L535 (reentrant `claim` self-nullifies to amount=0 via the L528-529 zero-skip); `MintRouterNotSet` guard L534 strictly more restrictive than pre-K; `setMintRouter` one-shot + `onlyGovernance` L224-229 (mis-bind = liveness DoS, not inflation — bounded by AuMM's one-shot minter); differential INV-1/INV-6 preserved vs `stage-j-complete` | could-not-assess: INV-2 schedule conservation (see next line); deploy mintRouter/minter wiring order (→WK.17); hostile-router binding (→WK.4).

**WK.5 · S1 supply/mint integrity · `src/emission/BodenseeBootstrapChannel.sol` (bootstrap-flush mint via router) — NO FINDING (K-diff surface).**
- WK.5 S1: NO FINDING (K-diff surface) | checked: sole mint site `distribute` L267 `mintRouter.mintFor` → router → AuMM cap L114 (router-wiring faithful); conservation double-backstop (reserve-delta L307-308 + bptOut==0 L306 + residual balanceOf==0 L276-277 → no stranded mint; atomic rollback on callback failure); reentrancy double-belt (onlyGovernance L261 + CEI pendingAccrual=0 L264 → reentrant `distribute` reverts NoPendingAccrual L263 + `_EXECUTING_SLOT` transient guard L268); AP math bounded + underflow-safe L239-253; single rate snapshot exact (bootstrap window entirely within Era 0); differential INV-1/INV-6 preserved | could-not-assess: INV-2 cross-contract conservation (see observation); real-Vault `distribute` fork path (→WK.18); deploy wiring (→WK.17).

Whether the hand-off conserves (no overlap → double-mint, no gap) is a CROSS-CONTRACT seam neither WK.3 nor WK.5 can settle in isolation. Still unaudited, no `ReferenceEmission` harness. The 21M cap bounds total emission (no infinite inflation); a split error would misallocate LP-vs-Bodensee emission against the F-0 spec. Recommend the WH-H back-fill / Stage P sweep build an INV-2 harness summing channel + distributor mints against an independent reference.

**WK.9 · S3 rounding / invariant precision · `src/governance/AureumGovernance.sol` (vote tally, quorum, F-9 dampening) — NO FINDING on arithmetic; one SUSPECTED item raised.**
- WK.9 S3: NO FINDING on tally arithmetic (ZERO division in the entire contract → no rounding risk; quorum L289 / 2-of-3 supermajority L291 / simple majority L293 all cross-multiplied & integer-exact; deposit L179-183 constant lookup; no overflow for any plausible weight scale; F-9 dampening is upstream in `VOTING_WEIGHT.governanceWeight` L258 → WK.12) | SUSPECTED (first candidate finding of the pass; formal F-NN row deferred to WK.10 post-PoC): the quorum denominator is the propose-time snapshot `snapshotTotalSupply = VOTING_WEIGHT.totalSupply()` L201/L289, while vote-weight numerators accrue at vote-time (poke + governanceWeight L257-258) — diverges from the standard same-block Governor snapshot; permissive direction when global voting supply grows over the ~14d (BLOCKS_PER_EPOCH) window; bounded by VotingWeight's 14d qualification cliff (blocks flash-vote) + 6mo on-ramp slope; MAY be intended per I-D17 veto-denominator semantics.
- Routing — exploitability PoC at WK.10 (S9: a proposer picks a low-supply propose block + late-window high-weight vote; quantify the quorum-gaming gain within the cliff/on-ramp limits); intent/spec confirmation at WK.13 (VotingWeight `totalSupply` / I-D17). Resolves to Confirmed (Low-Med quorum-gaming) or Accepted-risk / False-positive.
- Harness — quorum/tally boundary tests green in the 43/43 run (`test_state_quorumBoundaryStrict`, `supermajorityExactPass`, `belowSupermajorityDefeated`, `gauge_tieDefeated`, `fee_simpleMajorityPass`).
**WK.10 · S9 economic/incentive · `src/governance/AureumGovernance.sol` (quorum-snapshot timing) — FINDING F-01 (Confirmed, Medium).**
- WK.10 S9: FINDING F-01 (resolves the WK.9 SUSPECTED item) | checked: propose-time snapshot `VOTING_WEIGHT.totalSupply()` L201 stored L204 vs vote-time numerator `poke`+`governanceWeight` L257-258 feeding quorum L289; proposer NOT poked in `_createProposal` L196-206 (absent from denominator) but self-pokes in `castVote` L257 (present in numerator) → turnout can exceed 100% of snapshot; root cause `VotingWeight._totalQualifiedWeight` lazy poke-accumulator L115-120 excludes never-poked qualified holders; PoC 4/4 green proves all three proposal types pass under a shrunken/zero snapshot | could-not-assess (→WK.13): whether the propose-time snapshot is intended per I-D17 veto-denominator semantics (`VotingWeight.totalSupply` was specced for `VaultClassRegistry.vetoProposal`, not as an AureumGovernance quorum base) — F-01 stands on behavior regardless; resolution (code fix vs Accepted-risk) follows WK.13.
- Quantified — effective quorum = 20% of poked weight, not eligible weight; leverage ρ = poked/eligible is attacker-influenceable down (don't poke honest holders; poke withdrawn holders down) and structurally excludes the attacker's own weight; at ρ→0 (early protocol) quorum is vacuous and a single 1e18 vote carries.
- Bounds (→ Medium, not High) — 14-day qualification cliff (no flash weight, VotingWeight L137); non-refundable 1,000 svZCHF / 1,250 sUSDS deposit; ~16-day vote+timelock public contest (one larger honest against-vote defeats simple majority, ≥half defeats the 2/3 composition); permissionless poking keeps the accumulator honest; WK.8 bounded blast radius (no theft, governance-reversible). Rises toward High under sustained thin participation.
- Fix direction (resolution commit, post-WK.13) — read the quorum denominator at tally-time `endBlock` so all self-poked voters are included (turnout ≤ 100%), or adopt Governor-style historical weight checkpoints.
- Harness — `test/whitehat/F01_quorumSnapshotTiming.t.sol` 4/4 green: turnoutCanExceedSnapshot (forVotes 10_000e18 > snapshot 100e18), minorityAttackerRevokesGauge (200e18 ≈ 0.4% of true weight revokes a gauge), vacuousQuorumWhenAccumulatorEmpty (snapshot 0), singleVoterClearsCompositionSupermajority (100e18 clears 2/3 + executes the slot swap).
**WK.11 · S6 reentrancy & read-only reentrancy · `src/governance/AureumGovernance.sol` — NO FINDING.**
- WK.11 S6: NO FINDING | checked: castVote CEI — `hasVoted[id][msg.sender]=true` L256 set BEFORE the sole external call `VOTING_WEIGHT.poke` L257 → reentrant castVote reverts AlreadyVoted L255; execute CEI — `p.executed=true` L327 set BEFORE `_executeProposal` L328 (all external calls live there) → reentrant execute reverts ProposalAlreadyExecuted L322; queue has zero external calls (eta set + emit only) L307-313; `_createProposal` external calls (`safeTransferFrom` L198 + `BODENSEE_CHANNEL.donate` L199) precede `++proposalCount` L200 + snapshot L201, payToken restricted to SVZCHF/SUSDS immutables (`_depositAmount` reverts otherwise L179-183 — no attacker-controlled token), donate is `onlyAuthorizedDonator` + runs inside `_vault.unlock` (Vault nonReentrant); no receive/fallback/delegatecall/selfdestruct/assembly/low-level-call in the contract; all callees (VOTING_WEIGHT, GAUGE_REGISTRY, SLOT_REGISTRY, VAULT, BODENSEE_CHANNEL) are trusted protocol contracts; read-only reentrancy — the view surface (state/getProposal/hasVoted) reads consistent frozen state during execute (executed=true pre-set, tally final post-endBlock, eta set), and `snapshotTotalSupply` is the poke-accumulator value not a live TVL/price read, so no stale-read window | could-not-assess: whether `GAUGE_REGISTRY.registerGaugeFromComposition(newPool)` L344 callbacks into an attacker-controlled `newPool` during composition execute (GaugeRegistry = Stage G, not K-touched → WH-G; bounded regardless by the executed-flag re-execution block + frozen tally); real-Vault reentrancy of `VAULT.setStaticSwapFeePercentage` L348 + the donate→`_vault.unlock`→`addLiquidity` Bodensee path + der Bodensee add-liquidity hook reachability → WK.18 / WH-D.
- Observation — a benign reentrant `propose*` during the donate `_vault.unlock` callback is possible in principle but harmless: each proposal pays its own non-refundable deposit and gets an independent `++proposalCount` id + own snapshot; no shared-state corruption, no double-spend.
- Observation — the propose-time deposit donation (Vault.unlock + addLiquidity to der Bodensee, L199 → `SwapAndDepositToBodensee` L376/L411) mutates Bodensee TVL before the L201 snapshot read, but `snapshotTotalSupply` is `_totalQualifiedWeight` (poke-accumulator) not a TVL read, so there is no read-only-reentrancy coupling between the donation and the quorum base (orthogonal to F-01).
- Harness — no new PoC; the castVote/execute CEI revert guards are exercised in the K4.6 cohort (`AureumGovernance.t.sol` L401 AlreadyVoted + L603 ProposalAlreadyExecuted) within the 43/43 unit run.
**WK.12 · S9 economic/incentive · `src/governance/VotingWeight.sol` (F-9 weight integrity, gauge-gaming) — FINDING F-02 (Confirmed, Medium); 3 sub-surfaces clean.**
- WK.12 S9: FINDING F-02 + clean(flash/on-ramp/gauge) | checked clean: flash-loan resistance — value is recorder share `tvl·userLP/poolTotalLP` (hook-recorded deposits, not spot BPT, L138-142) + 14-day cliff `timeInPool<QUALIFICATION_PERIOD_BLOCKS→0` L137 → transient capital confers 0 (OQ-25 holds); on-ramp/cliff timing — `cappedTime=min(timeInPool,ON_RAMP)` L144-146 monotonic in held blocks, withdrawal resets `effectiveQualBlock` (no carry) → no time-gaming; gauge gate — unapproved pools confer 0 L133 | FINDING F-02: per-position concave root `base.powDown(e)` L152 (e=0.25 Era0 L149-151) is subadditive → splitting LP across N addresses multiplies summed `governanceWeight` by N^(1-e); PoC `test/whitehat/F02_concaveSplitSybil.t.sol` 3/3 green (16-split ≈8×, 81-split ≈27× a single consolidated position of identical LP) | could-not-assess (→WH-F + spec): whether F-9 Sybil-splittability is an accepted spec tradeoff (`11_formulas.md` F-9 / `03_theoretical_foundation.md` CCB / I-D7); whether the same concave split amplifies CCB/emission weight (EmissionDistributor/CCBMultiplier, Stage F/H, not K-scoped → WH-F/WH-H).
- Mechanism — x^e (e<1, f(0)=0) subadditive: Σf(pieces) ≥ f(Σ); value conserved across split (`tvl·Σlp_i/totalLP`, L142) but summed power amplified by N^(1-e); cliff/on-ramp/gauge identical across pieces (cancel); concavity per-(pool,holder) summed per holder (poke L110-112), no cross-address aggregation possible on-chain w/o identity → free Sybil multiplier on the same real capital.
- Bounds (→ Medium) — per-address gas + N-wallet ops (only real friction, scales with N); 14-day cliff (genuine non-flash capital held); value/base dust floor L143/L148 (very low); no on-chain Sybil resistance; bounded blast radius per WK.8 (no theft, governance-reversible); compounds F-01 (both lower the governance-capture bar).
- Fix direction (resolution, post-WH-F intent) — split-invariant form: apply the concave root to pool aggregate value distributed pro-rata by LP share (`share·poolValue^e`), preserving cross-pool concavity while being within-pool split-invariant; alternatively accept the concave-voting Sybil tradeoff explicitly. Spec-level F-9 change either way.
- Harness — `test/whitehat/F02_concaveSplitSybil.t.sol` 3/3 green: singleConsolidatedPositionWeight (positive baseline), split16xGivesApprox8xWeight (7×–9× bracket = 16^¾), split81xGivesApprox27xWeight (24×–30× bracket = 81^¾).
**WK.13 · S9 intent/spec confirmation · `VotingWeight.totalSupply()` semantics (I-D17) — F-01 intent RESOLVED: deviation, not intended reuse.**
- WK.13 intent: F-01 resolves to a DEVIATION from documented design intent, not an Accepted I-D17 reuse | evidence: (1) I-D17 (STAGE_I_NOTES L179) explicitly DEFERRED the `totalSupply()` veto-denominator semantics to "the Stage K snapshot layer", preserving only the function shape — the snapshot choice is a Stage-K (K4) decision, not an inherited I-D17 constraint; (2) VotingWeight NatSpec L13-14/L21 documents the design invariant `vetoSupport ≤ totalSupply` ("exact veto fraction … by construction"); (3) the original consumer `VaultClassRegistry.vetoProposal` PRESERVES it — reads `governanceWeight(msg.sender)` WITHOUT poke (L277, the checkpoint already summed into the accumulator) + `totalSupply()` LIVE at evaluation (L279), so numerator+denominator share one accumulator → support ≤ totalSupply structurally; (4) AureumGovernance BREAKS it — stores a propose-time `snapshotTotalSupply` (L201/L204) used as a stale denominator (L289) WHILE poke-refreshing the voter at vote-time (L257) above that stale base → forVotes can exceed snapshot (F-01 turnout>100%). The combination (stale stored denominator + refreshed numerator) is the divergence; either alone preserves the invariant.
- Resolution — F-01 stays Confirmed/Open, disposition CODE FIX (not Accepted-risk). Invariant-preserving fix mirroring vetoProposal: compute quorum against `totalSupply()` read LIVE at tally-time `endBlock` (by then every voter has self-poked, so forVotes ≤ totalSupply is restored). VotingWeight has no historical checkpoints, so a propose-time-snapshot-of-both is infeasible; the tally-time live read is the feasible alignment.
- Observation (corroborating) — empty-accumulator handling diverges: vetoProposal's live div-form L279 `(vetoSupport*10_000)/totalSupply()` REVERTS at totalSupply()==0 (fails closed, no spurious veto), while AureumGovernance's stored mul-form L289 `totalVotes*10_000 < snapshot*QUORUM_BPS` PASSES vacuously at snapshot==0 (F-01 `test_vacuousQuorumWhenAccumulatorEmpty`) — the mul-form's permissive direction is the unsafe choice.
- Observation (adjacent, →WH-G) — vetoProposal's L279 division-by-`totalSupply()` reverts (veto DoS, fails closed) on an empty accumulator; Stage-G contract, not K-diff — noted for the WH-G back-fill, not raised as a finding here.
- F-02 intent — NOT resolved here; the F-9 concave-Sybil spec-acceptance question stays routed to WH-F + the canonical spec (`11_formulas.md` F-9), distinct from F-01's totalSupply-snapshot intent. Constitution-level snapshot-voting intent (10_constitution §xxix) not fetched this step; the in-repo documented invariant is authoritative and already violated, which is sufficient to confirm the deviation.
**WK.14 · S3 rounding / invariant-precision · `src/governance/VotingWeight.sol` — NO FINDING.**
- WK.14 S3: NO FINDING | checked: exact-fraction accumulator invariant `_totalQualifiedWeight == Σ _holderWeight[*]` — `poke` adjusts the accumulator by EXACTLY the signed delta `newWeight-oldWeight` (L116-120) so the sum is preserved across re-pokes/withdrawals; no-op short-circuit `newWeight==oldWeight→return` L114 correct; underflow-safe — the subtraction arm `_totalQualifiedWeight -= oldWeight-newWeight` L119 cannot underflow because the invariant guarantees `_totalQualifiedWeight ≥ _holderWeight[holder]=oldWeight` (oldWeight is one summand) → result ≥ newWeight ≥ 0 (NatSpec L100-103); accumulator update is pure integer add/sub (no rounding drift); F12/F13 signed-delta discipline (branch-on-sign, unsigned sub per arm) holds; rounding UNIFORMLY *Down — `value` L142 / `base` L147 / `powDown` L152 round toward zero → a holder's weight is only ever UNDER-stated, never inflated; zero mulUp/divUp/powUp in the file (grep-confirmed) → no mixed-rounding asymmetry → safe for voting (voter can't round own weight up) + veto (consistent rounding preserves `vetoSupport ≤ totalSupply`); per-holder weight = Σ over ≤28 pools of base^e, base bounded by realistic TVL, base^¼ tiny → no uint256 overflow | could-not-assess: ORACLE.tvl precision/manipulation (TVLOracle = Stage H, black-box here → S7 / WH-H); powDown exact rounding bound on extreme inputs (Balancer LogExpMath, trusted audited primitive → differential vs upstream if needed).
- Observation — `value = tvl.mulDown(lp.divDown(totalLP))` L142 is division-before-multiplication (share fraction first, then multiply); minor precision loss vs `(tvl·lp)/totalLP` but always DOWN (conservative) and negligible for realistic values; not a finding.
- Observation — sub-1e-18 LP share rounds `value`/`base` to 0 → 0 weight (dust floor L143/L148); denies dust weight (safe direction, never grants), and bounds the F-02 split-piece from below (a Sybil piece must clear the dust floor to confer any weight).
- Harness — no new PoC; the F12/F13 signed-delta + exact-fraction invariant is directly exercised by the K3 cohort (`VotingWeight.t.sol` test_Poke_TotalSupply_InvariantHolds L339, test_Poke_TopUp_PositiveDelta L356, test_Poke_WithdrawalReset_NegativeDelta L329, test_Poke_Noop_WhenWeightUnchanged L314) within the 29-green unit run.

**Resolution · F-01 + F-02 + F-03 + F-04 — all four findings fixed (2026-06-12, post-close).**
- All four findings are resolved on `stage-k-whitehat` ahead of the `stage-k-complete` tag. The WK.1–WK.19 step blocks + the WK.19 roll-up record the as-of-close state (none Fixed); the findings table above carries the live `Fixed` status with fix hashes.
- **F-01** (`e6d9b3d`) — `AureumGovernance._voteSucceeded` reads the quorum denominator `VOTING_WEIGHT.totalSupply()` LIVE at tally (L294) instead of the propose-time `snapshotTotalSupply`, mirroring the WK.13 `vetoProposal` invariant-preserving form. Regression `test_F01_liveTallySupplyDefeatsStaleQuorumGaming` (accumulator grows 20× post-propose → minority vote Defeated); `F01_quorumSnapshotTiming.t.sol` 5/5.
- **F-03** (`1e84bfb`) — `DeployStageK._wire` gains the 9th call `tvlOracle.setMiliariumRegistry` (after the handoff, before `setAuthorizer`), binding the K6 Leg-2 enumeration and self-sealing `registrySetter` on deploy. Regression `test_F03_tvlOracleMiliariumRegistryBoundAndSealed`; `DeployStageK.t.sol` 6/6.
- **F-04** (`c283371` + `3d80ac8`) — `VotingWeight._positionPower` swaps spot `ORACLE.tvl` for the 60-day `EMASampler.tvlEMA` behind an `EMA_MATURITY_BLOCKS` (60-day) seed-age gate (`emaSeedBlock`), so an unseeded or immature EMA confers zero weight — closing the atomic spot-pump and the F-D15 cold-start residual; a mature EMA keeps the F-9 quartic bound. `emaSeedBlock` is an additive Stage-F fix-forward (I13-class, no re-tag). Regression: `F04_spotTvlManipulation.t.sol` flipped to prove the gate (4/4) + `EMASampler.t.sol` seed-block tests (27/27); full regression 778/778 unit + 94/94 fork.
- **F-02** (`a5a3a1b` + aumm-site `50e21a5`) — the spec-gated pool-aggregate-root fix landed both-sided: canonical F-9 rewritten first (aumm-site `11_formulas.md` + the 04/10/12/17 consistency sweep, `50e21a5`), then `VotingWeight._positionPower` → `tvlEMA^era-root × LP-share × linear timeFactor`. The holder leg is linear, so wallet-splitting is weight-invariant (split sums to consolidated-minus-rounding-dust); subadditivity migrates to the gauge-gated pool axis (≤ roster size, real diversified LP — incentive-aligned, spec-accepted). PoC flipped to fix-proving (`F02_concaveSplitSybil.t.sol` 3/3); unit recalibration 32/32; F04 ratios stable 4/4; regression 778/778 unit + 94/94 fork.
- None of the four fixes touches the Vault substrate seam — `Vault.sol`/`VaultAdmin.sol`/`VaultExtension.sol` byte-identity preserved (CLAUDE.md §1).
