## Fix design — F-06 (Stage-L) · Governor-checkpoint snapshot voting

> Locks the chosen fix for F-06 (row above, Medium, Suspected, Open) BEFORE implementation, per the §12 ambiguity-gate. SUPERSEDES the WK-R.8 disposition sketch ("snapshot the quorum DENOMINATOR at `endBlock`"): that lazy-capture froze only the denominator and left a one-block `endBlock` capture surface; the chosen fix freezes BOTH numerator and denominator at a single pre-vote `snapshotBlock`, closing the F-06 inflation grief AND the WK-R.8 symmetric deflate-to-pass face, and converting F-01's live-read into a structural numerator ≤ denominator invariant. Recorded here, not as a K-D entry — a new K-D would mislabel a Stage-L whitehat fix as pre-tag build-line design. Status stays Open until the code + PoC land; the resolution commit then flips the F-06 row to Fixed with the hash + test path per the two-commit discipline below. Fix-forward on `stage-l`, no Stage-K re-tag (I13-class add-on, F-01..F-04 precedent). OZ `Checkpoints.Trace208` / `upperLookup` / `SafeCast` signatures are confirmed at the first `VotingWeight` `forge build` (G10/G14 compile-probe discipline).

**Design class — Governor-style historical checkpointing.** `VotingWeight` gains per-holder + total weight HISTORY; `AureumGovernance` reads voter weight and the quorum denominator at a frozen `snapshotBlock = startBlock + VOTING_DELAY_BLOCKS`, so post-snapshot `poke`s move neither side.

1. **`VotingWeight` checkpoint history.** Add two OZ `Checkpoints.Trace208` traces (`lib/openzeppelin-contracts/contracts/utils/structs/Checkpoints.sol`, pinned 5.6.1) — one per-holder, one for the running total. `poke` pushes both on every non-no-op delta: key `block.number` → `SafeCast.toUint48`, value the new weight → `SafeCast.toUint208` (reverts on overflow, never truncates). Add `getPastVotes(address holder, uint256 blockNumber)` + `getPastTotalSupply(uint256 blockNumber)` via `upperLookup`, each reverting `FutureLookup(blockNumber)` when `blockNumber >= block.number`. The live `governanceWeight` / `totalSupply` / `poke` surface is UNCHANGED — the veto path keeps live reads (see scope).
   - Bound: `_positionPower` weight is `tvlEMA^(1/4..1/3) · share · timeFactor` ≈ ≤1e21/holder, total ≪ `uint208` max (≈4.1e62) — `SafeCast` is the fail-closed belt, not an expected path.
   - Invariant: the total trace is pushed with the running sum on every `poke`, so its value at any block = Σ holders' checkpointed weight at that block; therefore Σ over voters of `getPastVotes(h, snap)` ≤ `getPastTotalSupply(snap)` — numerator ≤ denominator BY CONSTRUCTION. This is the F-06 closure and the durable F-01 fix.

2. **`AureumGovernance` snapshot + voting delay.** `VOTING_DELAY_BLOCKS = AureumTime.BLOCKS_PER_DAY` (7_200, ~1 day; parity with `EXECUTION_TIMELOCK_BLOCKS = 2 · BLOCKS_PER_DAY`). Struct `Proposal`: REPLACE `snapshotTotalSupply` (uint256) → `snapshotBlock` (uint256), 1-for-1, no slot-layout growth. `_createProposal` sets `snapshotBlock = start + VOTING_DELAY_BLOCKS` and `end = snapshotBlock + VOTING_PERIOD_BLOCKS`, and DROPS the live `VOTING_WEIGHT.totalSupply()` read. `ProposalCreated` emits `snapshotBlock` in place of `snapshotTotalSupply`. Enum `ProposalState`: APPEND `Pending` (new value 6 — preserves Active=0..Expired=5 for existing raw-uint consumers).

3. **`state()` ordering.** `Executed` → `Pending` (`block.number < snapshotBlock`) → `Active` (`snapshotBlock <= block.number <= endBlock`) → post-`endBlock` tally (`_voteSucceeded` → `Defeated` / `Succeeded` / `Queued` / `Expired`). Adds the lower bound at `snapshotBlock` that today's check (`block.number <= endBlock` only) lacks. Non-existent proposals (all-zero fields) keep deriving to `Defeated` (the `block.number < snapshotBlock` test is false at `snapshotBlock == 0`, so they fall through to the tally path).

4. **`castVote` / `_voteSucceeded` snapshot reads.** `castVote`: gate on Active (`block.number < snapshotBlock || block.number > endBlock` → `ProposalNotActive`); REMOVE the `VOTING_WEIGHT.poke(msg.sender)` call; read `weight = VOTING_WEIGHT.getPastVotes(msg.sender, p.snapshotBlock)` (`snapshotBlock < block.number` holds inside Active, so never `FutureLookup`). `hasVoted` CEI guard unchanged. `_voteSucceeded`: denominator reads `VOTING_WEIGHT.getPastTotalSupply(p.snapshotBlock)` in place of live `totalSupply()`.

5. **`IVotingWeight`.** Add `getPastVotes` + `getPastTotalSupply`; keep the existing three members. The `poke` NatSpec ("called on `msg.sender` immediately before reading `governanceWeight`") is now STALE — `castVote` no longer pokes; rewrite that NatSpec to the snapshot semantics in the interface sub-step.

**Load-bearing semantics (bake into Must-match at materialization):**
- **Pre-snapshot `poke` is load-bearing.** A voter's counted weight is the last checkpoint AT OR BEFORE `snapshotBlock`; a `poke` during the Active window updates live state + future checkpoints but NOT this proposal's vote. The ~1-day delay is the establish-weight window; permissionless third-party `poke` (K-D5) still works. This AMENDS K-D6b's "poke-at-vote" semantics, justified by the whitehat fix.
- **+1-day lifecycle.** Propose→close grows from ~14 days (`VOTING_PERIOD_BLOCKS`) to ~15 (delay + period) — intentional Governor-delay extension of K-D6f, not drift.
- **G16 blast radius — exactly three mocks** (`grep -rn "is IVotingWeight"`): `test/unit/AureumGovernance.t.sol`, `test/unit/VaultClassRegistry.t.sol`, `test/fork/mocks/StageGMocks.sol` — each gains stub `getPastVotes` / `getPastTotalSupply`. Re-grep at the interface sub-step per G16.
- **`ProposalCreated` field change** — `snapshotTotalSupply` → `snapshotBlock`; the AureumGovernance test event assert + any off-chain indexer update with it.

**Scope boundary (confirmed).** F-06 stays scoped to `AureumGovernance`. `VaultClassRegistry.vetoProposal` (G-D9, `stage-g-complete`) keeps its LIVE `governanceWeight` / `totalSupply` reads — a different consumer (cumulative accrual over `VETO_WINDOW_BLOCKS`, no `poke`, no post-`endBlock` frozen-numerator geometry), so the F-06 attack does not exist there. The veto path's live-denominator surface is logged as a SEPARATE carry-forward observation (→ a future `F-NN` if tracked), not folded into F-06's blast radius.

**Materialization order (one §8e.1 each, F-06 before F-05):** (i) `VotingWeight` checkpoint history + `getPast*`; (ii) `IVotingWeight` two members + `poke` NatSpec rewrite; (iii) `AureumGovernance` struct / enum / `_createProposal` / `state` / `castVote` / `_voteSucceeded`; (iv) the three `is IVotingWeight` mocks; (v) F-06 PoC `test/whitehat/F06_*.t.sol` (post-snapshot `poke` no longer moves the tally) + regression; (vi) resolution commit flips the F-06 row → Fixed.

**RESOLVED (2026-06-14, Stage-L) — the F-06 findings-table row above is flipped Open→Fixed.** Landed: (i) `VotingWeight` `Checkpoints.Trace208` weight history + `getPastVotes`/`getPastTotalSupply` (`9b57d9c`); (ii) `IVotingWeight` two members + `VotingWeight` override + three `MockVotingWeight` stubs (`8fdd476`); (iii)+(iv) `AureumGovernance` snapshot voting (`ProposalState.Pending` + `VOTING_DELAY_BLOCKS`; numerator `getPastVotes(voter, snapshotBlock)` and denominator `getPastTotalSupply(snapshotBlock)` both frozen at `snapshotBlock`; `poke` dropped from `castVote`) plus the `AureumGovernance.t.sol` snapshot-window re-alignment and the `F01_quorumSnapshotTiming.t.sol` re-point (`97131f8`); (v) the block-aware PoC `test/whitehat/F06_postEndBlockDenominatorFreeze.t.sol` (`427187a`). Execution diverged from the planned split: the (iv) mocks folded into (ii), and (iii)+(iv) committed as one unit. SUPERSESSION — snapshot voting removes F-01's `e6d9b3d` live-`totalSupply()` denominator, so the historical F-01 records (the findings-table F-01 row and the WK.10/WK.13 step blocks) describe the now-superseded pre-F-06 mechanism; they are preserved as the dated record, reconciled here. The F01 suite was re-pointed from bug/live-read demos to snapshot regressions and renamed: `turnoutCanExceedSnapshot` → `test_F01_turnoutBoundedBySnapshotSupply`; `minorityAttackerRevokesGauge` → `test_F01_dustAttackerCannotRevokeGauge`; `singleVoterClearsCompositionSupermajority` → `test_F01_singleVoterCannotClearComposition`; `liveTallySupplyDefeatsStaleQuorumGaming` → `test_F01_snapshotDenominatorDefeatsStaleQuorumGaming`; `vacuousQuorumWhenAccumulatorEmpty` → `test_F06_vacuousQuorumAtZeroSnapshotStillOpen` (the `snapshot==0` vacuous mul-form is NOT closed by F-06 — orthogonal to the denominator-timing axis, still Open per the WK.13 L140 observation, carried to Stage-P). Regression: F06 2/2 + F01 5/5 + `AureumGovernance` 43/43, 780 non-fork tests green.

## Fix — F-05 (Stage-L) · EMA freshness gate

**RESOLVED (2026-06-15, Stage-L) — the F-05 findings-table row above is flipped Open→Fixed.** Landed: (i) the `VotingWeight._positionPower` freshness gate + shared-mock retrofit (`d5cecb3`); (ii) the PoC `test/whitehat/F05_emaFreshnessGate.t.sol` (`70eb276`). The fix adds `EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS = AureumTime.BLOCKS_PER_EPOCH` (100_800, 14 days / one epoch) and the gate `if (block.number - EMA_SAMPLER.lastEMAUpdateBlock(pool) > EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS) return 0;` immediately after the seed-AGE maturity gate (`VotingWeight` L167, after the `seedBlock == 0` guard at L165). `EMA_MATURITY_BLOCKS` (60 days) now gates seed AGE; the new constant gates seed FRESHNESS — so both faces named in the finding are closed: the F-05 base 13.5%-residual decay path (a stale inflated seed only survives if it is ALSO refreshed within the epoch, but an honest refresh moves the EMA toward spot) AND the WK-R.6-sharpened 100%-inflated pre-approval dormant-seed window (a seed never re-sampled has `block.number - lastEMAUpdateBlock` growing without bound, so it is structurally stale the instant the 60-day clock matures → 0). `lastEMAUpdateBlock == 0` (never updated) is fail-closed stale. CHOSEN PATH — the consumer-side freshness gate over the seed-path smoothing/anchor alternative named in the disposition: it is the minimal fail-closed surface (one external read + one compare in `_positionPower`), leaves `EMASampler` byte-untouched, and is shared by every `poke` caller so both the snapshot-vote and the live-veto consumers inherit it. I13 blast radius — because the gate is reached by all `poke` callers, the shared `MockEMASampler` (`test/unit/VotingWeight.t.sol`) gained a `setLastUpdateBlock` setter, the `_configurePosition` unit helper stamps `block.number`, and the F02 / F04 PoC `setUp`s stamp the pool fresh; all retrofitted in the (i) commit, no test regressed. PoC `test/whitehat/F05_emaFreshnessGate.t.sol` (4/4) pins both faces and the strict-`>` boundary: a deeply-stale mature EMA confers 0, a fresh mature EMA confers weight, delta == EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS still flows, delta == +1 zeroes. Regression: F05 4/4 + F04 4/4 + F02 3/3 + `VotingWeight` 32/32, 784 non-fork tests green. Fix-forward on `stage-l` (I13-class, no Stage-K re-tag). With F-05 and F-06 both Fixed, the WK-R pass carries ZERO open findings forward — the WK-R.10 close-out attestations (rows above: 'both Suspected + Open + disposition-routed') are preserved as the dated record of the pass and reconciled here.

## Fix — F-07 (Stage-L) · IncendiaryRegistry EMA freshness gate

**RESOLVED (2026-06-17, Stage-L) — recorded directly as Fixed.** Unlike F-05/F-06 (recorded Open in the WK-R pass, then later Fixed), F-07 was surfaced and fixed within the same scheduled Stage-L spot review, so the findings-table row above is recorded directly as Fixed; the source fix + test retrofit (`0a21b99`) and the dedicated PoC (`b96227c`) predate this ledger entry. The fix adds `EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS = AureumTime.BLOCKS_PER_EPOCH` (100_800, 14 days / one epoch, `IncendiaryRegistry` L47) and the gate `if (block.number - rail.lastSampleBlock > EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS) revert EMAStale(payToken, staleness, EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS);` (L337-338) immediately after the seed-AGE maturity gate (`seedBlock == 0` → EMANotMature L334; `age < EMA_MATURITY_BLOCKS` → EMANotMature L336). `EMA_MATURITY_BLOCKS` (432_000, 60 days, L44) gates seed AGE; the new constant gates seed FRESHNESS — so a mature-but-stale der-Bodensee price-EMA rail (seasoned ≥60d but never re-sampled, `block.number - lastSampleBlock` growing without bound) is structurally stale the instant the 60-day clock matures and reverts rather than pricing a `buyBoost`. `lastSampleBlock` advances only on permissionless `updateRailEMA`, so the gate is anyone-can-refresh and fail-closed. MIRRORS F-05 (the source NatSpec at L46 labels it the 'F-05 anti-stale-seed floor') — same constant name, same one-epoch threshold, same consumer-side placement — but DIFFERS in degradation: F-05's `VotingWeight` consumer returns 0 weight (a stale position silently does not count), whereas the pricing path REVERTS `EMAStale` (a stale EMA must not silently misprice a purchase; the buy fails closed). DISTINCT ID, not F-05-extended — a different EMA consumer (der-Bodensee PRICE EMA for the boost deposit→entitlement valuation, rate-scaled-LINEAR, vs F-05's TVL EMA for quartically-damped governance weight) in a different contract, so a monotonic F-07 per the ID rule; opens the WL lineage the WK-R scope reserved for Stage L. I13 blast radius — the gate is a fail-fast check inside `_maturePrice`, reached by EVERY caller (`buyBoost` via `_valueInAuMM`, plus the `extMaturePrice` / `extValueInAuMM` harness wrappers), so the F-07.2 retrofit added a fresh `updateRailEMA` re-sample before each pre-existing mature-rail test (the initial buyBoost-only survey missed the harness-wrapper paths, surfaced at unit regression, closed in `_seedAndMature` + `test_extMaturePrice_mature`) plus a new `test_buyBoost_revert_emaStale` negative. PoC `test/whitehat/F07_emaStalePricing.t.sol` (4/4) pins the triad: a deeply-stale mature EMA reverts `EMAStale`, a fresh mature EMA prices, staleness == `EMA_STALENESS_BLOCKS` still flows (strict `>`), staleness == +1 reverts. Regression: 825/825 unit + 99/99 fork green. Fix-forward on `main` (Stage-L add-on, I13-class, no re-tag).

## WK.18 could-not-assess — CLOSED (Stage P-bis / PB2.11) · populated-roster fork sim

**CLOSED-BY-EVIDENCE (2026-07-15, Stage P-bis / PB2.11 per PB-D16) — the WK.18 F-04 could-not-assess (the full economic exploit: a cheap thin-venue oracle pump beating the quartic damping in a sparse roster, R1-blocked at Stage K on `tvl=0` pilots and routed to a populated-roster fork sim) is closed with fork-grade evidence. F-04 itself stays Fixed (the 60-day `EMASampler.tvlEMA` maturity + freshness gate on `VotingWeight._positionPower`, `c283371` + `3d80ac8`); this record adds the populated-roster confirmation the R1 block had deferred — no finding-status change.** The evidence is `test/fork/WK18ThinVenuePumpSim.t.sol` (PB2.11b-i…vi, 6/6 fork-green): a `StageNIntegrationFixture`-based sim on the REAL mainnet-fork Vault with the full governance-weight stack (real `VotingWeight` + `TVLOracle` + `EMASampler` + the 21-pool populated `MiliariumRegistry`), two hookless USDC/svZChf `addConstellationPool` venues, and a gauge-seeded, recorder-tracked pilot carrying a REAL 60-day-matured EMA — no `_mockPoolEma`, the StageK shortcut the R1 `tvl=0` block had forced. Three faces close the sub-questions the sparse-roster gap left open: (1) POSITIVE CONTROL (`WK18Face1PositiveControlTest`) — an atomic same-tx venue pump moves spot `tvl(scoredPool)` but leaves `governanceWeight` BYTE-IDENTICAL (`assertEq`), the fork-grade proof that post-fix `_positionPower` reads the gated EMA, not spot; (2) CROSS-VENUE DILUTION (`WK18Face2CrossVenueDilutionTest`) — a single-venue pump moves the `_directRatio` USDC mean by ~1/N (N=2), observed wei-exact on this run: both-venue delta `0.333149779698227576e18` = 2x single-venue delta `0.166574889849113788e18` off a `1.0e18` baseline (the test asserts the 1/N relation within 2% via `assertApproxEqRel`), so a populated roster strictly weakens the sparse-roster pump the R1 scenario feared, also carrying the WH-P6 `addConstellationPool` double-append attestation (governance-controlled + mean-damped, not a code path); (3) SUSTAINED-PUMP ECONOMICS (`WK18Face3SustainedPumpEconomicsTest`) — a 200%-of-depth svZChf venue pump inflates spot `tvl` +99.6% (9959 bps) but confers only +10.69% `governanceWeight` (1069 bps) after 21 daily `updateEMA` samples, because the 60-day EMA absorbs ~half the (spot minus EMA) gap in 21 days ((59/61)^21 = 0.4965) and the Era-0 quartic root compresses the absorbed portion, matching the closed-form (1.5035)^(1/4) = 1.1073 model to 4 bps → bounded fee-negative (`weightGainBps*2 = 2138 << spotInflationBps = 9959`, the PB2.4d sandwich-sim shape), with the on-ramp `timeFactor` confound isolated by a 130-day pre-roll pinning `timeFactor` at the 180-day `ON_RAMP` cap before measurement. The WH-H thin-venue `_constellationRatio` economics and the WH-P6 double-append folded threads exit attested-bounded within faces (2)/(3). Invocation: D35 split-form + D36 `--threads 1`. The whitehat ledger continues to carry zero open findings.

## Commit discipline

Follows the project's two-commit pattern:
1. **Finding commit** — adds/updates the row(s) in this ledger.
2. **Resolution commit** — the fix in code, referenced by hash in the **Fix**
   column, plus the row's Status moved to `Fixed`.

The PoC test lands with the fix (a fix without a regression test does not close
a finding). At a stage boundary, the coverage roll-up row for that pass must be
complete before tagging `stage-X-complete`.
